# Current Evidence Ledger — 2026-09-05

Companion to `GLM-FLASH-IMPLEMENTATION-AND-UI-PLAN-2026-09-05.md` and the historical
`COMPETITIVE-PARITY-AUDIT-2026-07-01.md`. One row per finding/package. States:
open / implementing / implemented-awaiting-review / verified / partial / unavailable / blocked-with-reason.
"Verified" requires an independent review of the acceptance evidence (the 2026-09-05 review session
verified commits through `d03d6e9` on main; everything after that is implemented-awaiting-review).

Dated corrections to stale historical claims (superseded by HEAD):
- Compare IS reachable (entry point wired, §9.10). OCR language selection exists (interactive + batch).
- Form auto-detect is a real content-aware heuristic, not three dummy fields (88d4286).
- JPEG re-encoding exists and is tested (4d8d3fb, TestCompressJpegReencode).
- Form-add undo exists despite an obsolete no-op comment (EditFormFieldCommand — see R02 for its gaps).
- README's 14-test count and the July plan's 39-test count are stale; current registration is 85 CTest
  targets on this branch (volatile — do not hardcode counts in prose going forward).

## Newly discovered engine defect (exposed by U05 fixtures)

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Evidence |
|----|---------|---------|--------|----------|
| E-1 | redaction excision backend | Redacting one line corrupts a byte-ADJACENT same-stream Tj (observed 'PUBLIC_KEEP_TEXT' -> 'PUBLIC_KEEP_XEXX') | **fixed** — root cause: PdfString::GetString() forces lazy glyph-encoding evaluation IN PLACE on the COW-shared operand; the stream rebuild re-emitted the transcoded (corrupted) form. getEncodedStringWidth now evaluates a deep raw copy (FromRaw), so un-redacted operators re-emit byte-exact; Edact-Ray gap-width property preserved | 2c5a0d6 — TestExcisionCorruption (same-page + 4-op stream, hex-level byte-identity asserts); 108/108 |

## Known test-infrastructure facts (Q02)

- A fresh checkout/worktree needs three untracked binary trees to build and run:
  `third_party/podofo/install/bin`, `third_party/pdfium/bin`, `onnxruntime-win-x64-1.17.3` —
  without them CMake silently falls back to MSYS2 podofo 0.10.4 (API mismatch) and tests exit
  0xc0000135. Documented in 05a3336's commit message; a bootstrap script remains open work.
- Full-suite runs raced twice with the post-commit graphify rebuild hook (transient failures
  resolved on rerun). Gate rule: ctest only when `cmake --build` is a no-op / hook log idle.

PyMuPDF/OpenCV/Pillow `infra` probe (`cleanup-results.json`). Each reproduced
its documented failure exactly; post-fix the SAME probes pass while every
non-owned marker (G07 geometry, G08 history, G10 OCR staleness, G12/G15
residuals) is unchanged. Probe harness + logs: `.context/evidence-2026-09-08/`
(`gateA-repro-*.log` pre-fix, `gateA-postfix-*.log` post-fix) and

(`gateA-revert-*-PREFIX.txt`: 3 failed in TestEngineSave, 3 in TestCleanupCli,

## 2026-09-09 merge integration repair — G05 × startup orphan prompt (feat/parity-glm)

The merged tree (G-A 7f8cf95 + Gate B 1e1ff16 + cleanup) hung
`TestRecoverySave::recoverySaveCommitsToOriginalAndCloseVerifies` (QtTest
300 s function timeout in-suite; ctest Timeout 120 s). Root cause, proven by
gdb attach (GUI thread parked in the dialog's event loop) and instrumented
markers (`.context/gateA-merge/`): recoverDocument() publishes the original to
recents while its recovery input is newer BY CONSTRUCTION; the deferred
startup orphan check (findOrphanedAutosaves "autosave newer" heuristic) then
flagged the LIVE pair and popped `RecoveryDialog.exec()` over the active
session. The pre-fix misfire was mtime-racy (NTFS quantization sometimes made
the fixture timestamps equal), which is why both merge parents were green and
the break appeared only at the merge. NOT a CheckedHistory/G12 interaction.

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Code | Regression test | Evidence | Commit | Residual |
|----|---------|---------|--------|------|-----------------|----------|--------|----------|
| G05-integration | MainWindow ctor orphan check × G05 recovery identity | The startup RecoveryDialog fired for the LIVE recovery pair recoverDocument had just published to recents (a recovery pair is autosave-newer by definition), modal over the active session — test hang; in production a modal over an in-progress recovery | implemented-awaiting-review | the startup orphan check suppresses ONLY the current session's live pair (DocumentSession::recoverySource non-empty → removeAll(path)); every genuine leftover still prompts, Discard still deletes those | TestRecoverySave: fixture original is BACKDATED 60 s (deterministic misfire — pre-fix was mtime-racy); a dismissal watchdog closes any startup prompt with Decide Later (never Discard; note close()/reject() maps to the dialog's Recover result) and records whether the live pair was listed — both test functions assert it never is | `.context/gateA-merge/`: gdb-hang.txt, inst-run1.txt (markers: prompt fires over live pair), prefix-run*.txt (3/3 deterministic failures listing the run's own fixture pair), postfix-run*.txt (5/5 pass), ctest-merged-final.log/run2/run3 | 9e4edab | genuine leftover orphans from killed runs still prompt at startup (by design); tests dismiss them with Later |

Full-suite gate at 9e4edab: `ctest -j 2` run 3 = **122/122, 100% passed**
(`ctest-merged-final-run3.log`; Gate B suites verified green in the same run:
TestHistoryIntegrity, TestFormUndo, TestBatchOpsCoverage truthful summaries,
TestMeasureRoundTrip, TestPageLabels, TestMeasurePanelHonesty). Runs 1–2 had
sub-second zero-output early deaths of TestEngineSave/TestFormSafety
immediately after the 707-step rebuild — pass standalone (12/12 each) and
under repeated ctest; same signature as the known TestReadOnlyGate cold-start
flake, unrelated surfaces.

## 2026-09-13 — R05/JS-01: form-JS execution deadline was bypassable (feat/parity-glm-r05)

The P1 blocker from PERFORMANCE-AND-SCRIPT-REVIEW-2026-09-10, reproduced by
the reviewer on b38a4fd and re-reproduced at this lane's tip (7ba5ca1): the
quickjs interrupt deadline was armed only around the single authored-script
eval — `deadlineMs = -1` before and after it — so every OTHER entry into the
engine ran script-reachable code with no budget at all.

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Code | Regression/gate + controls | Commit | Residual (explicit) |
|----|---------|---------|--------|------|----------------------------|--------|---------------------|

Three whole-project packages from WHOLE-ARCHITECTURE-REVIEW-2026-09-10 (A03, A06)
and WHOLE-PRODUCT-AND-PLAN-REVIEW-2026-09-10 (PP03), implemented at the shared
boundaries on top of everything through R06/R13. Base cbbb8ec (125-126/126);
fixes e0f3085 (R04), c10b20c (R08), 624c5a8 (R10). Evidence under
`.context/evidence-2026-09-08/r04r08r10-r04|r08|r10/` (source + runtime repros,
revert-verify captures, per-suite pass logs) and
`.context/evidence-2026-09-08/r04r08r10-final-ctest.log`.

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Code | Regression/gate + controls | Commit | Residual (explicit) |
|----|---------|---------|--------|------|----------------------------|--------|---------------------|
| WP-R04 | src/engines/SafeSave.{h,cpp}, src/shell/controllers/HomeController.cpp | A03: Share → Create encrypted package DELETED the destination (QFile::remove(outPath)) BEFORE launching 7-Zip and let the tool write the FINAL path under waitForFinished(-1) — a failed launch, a failed tool run, input disappearance or app exit destroyed the previous package; the wait was unbounded and uncancellable | implemented-awaiting-review | SafeSave gains runExternalWriterCommit — the SafeSave transaction shape extended to external writers: unique owned candidate (makeUniqueCandidate now takes a suffix; the 0-byte reservation is dropped before launch because `7z a` REFUSES a pre-existing empty archive — probed) → tool writes the CANDIDATE (the destination is never an argument) → validate (readable/non-empty + caller read-back) → checked atomic replace through the existing commitFileToDestination; candidate removed on every outcome. runBoundedProcess owns the process: bounded wait (default 120 s), cooperative cancel, KILL on cancel/deadline. HomeController::createEncryptedPackage drops the pre-delete, validates with a real `7z t -p<pwd>` read-back, adds a Cancel button that kills the 7-Zip process we own, and reports honest outcomes (canceled: "existing file was not modified"; failure: error + destination intact) | TestEncryptedPackageSafeWrite (NEW, 9 slots) drives the REAL transaction with a re-exec'd fake writer: launch failure, tool failure (exit 3, no output), partial output (candidate exists but exit≠0 → refused), validator refusal, injected CommitFault (FailBeforeCommit seam — destination byte-identical), success (candidate bytes replace destination, candidate cleaned), mid-flight cancel (writer killed via atomic flag, sentinel SHA-256 unchanged), deadline kill of a hung writer, plus a REAL 7-Zip end-to-end leg: sentinel survives a failing real run; success opens with the chosen password, REJECTS a wrong one, and extracts exactly the input bytes. Revert-verify: the suite cannot compile at pre-fix HEAD — the transaction seam did not exist (r04r08r10-r04/revert-verify-compile-fails-on-prefix.txt; pre-fix destructive sequence quoted in prefix-source-repro.txt) | e0f3085 | the package password still transits the 7-Zip command line (pre-existing; 7z header-encrypts the archive itself); cancellation between pages of a MULTI-STEP tool pipeline is the tool's own concern — the transaction guarantees the process we own is killed and nothing partial is committed; input existence is intentionally not pre-validated (a missing input is the tool's failure, reported with the sentinel intact); no sanitizer run claimed |
| WP-R08 | src/ui/PrintJob.{h,cpp} (NEW), src/ui/PdfViewerWidget.cpp (printDocument) | PP03: after QPrintDialog, printDocument() took the full page count and walked pages 0..N-1 — the dialog's page range/order was never read; each page was rendered by a fresh QThread capturing the LIVE view-owned QPdfDocument (switch/close mid-print is a lifetime risk; the destructor never joined workers); newPage() failure, null renders and painter state were ignored, and the cancel branch skipped the progress-dialog cleanup the normal branch ran | implemented-awaiting-review | gp::PrintJob is the ONE owned print job: pageSequence() maps QPrinter's printRange/fromPage/toPage/pageOrder to an explicit 0-based sequence (All/Selection→all documented, CurrentPage, PageRange clamped — inverted range prints NOTHING and is reported, never silently widened); the job owns an immutable snapshot — its OWN QPdfDocument opened on the displayed file path, no view object participates (job survives dialog-parent/view destruction mid-print); render workers touch only job-owned state; cooperative cancel at every page boundary; render failure, painter start failure, newPage/spool failure and the post-print printer error state each produce a truthful, VISIBLE error with exactly one terminal finished(printed, canceled, error). Qt trap defused: QProgressDialog::closeEvent EMITS canceled() (also on its automatic completion close) — autoClose/autoReset off + moot-cancel guard so a COMPLETED print is never reported canceled. PdfViewerWidget::printDocument sets the dialog min/max, builds the sequence from the dialog's choices, hands the snapshot path to the job, warns on failure and posts status text for every outcome | TestPrintJob (NEW, 9 slots) drives the ACTUAL paint path headlessly via QPrinter PdfFormat print-to-file: printedPdfContainsExactlyTheSelectedPages + printedPdfHonorsRangeAndOrder reopen the printed PDF and pin page count AND content by ink-normalized raster comparison (range {2,0} must print source pages 3 then 1 — argmin + separation against all source pages); pageSequence unit pins (clamps, inverted→empty, current page, LastPageFirst); jobSurvivesDialogParentDestructionMidPrint (view deleted mid-print, output still complete); cancelBeforeStart → NO output file + truthful canceled; unopenable printer/document → visible error, no partial artifact. Revert-verify: cannot build at pre-fix HEAD — the job seam did not exist (r04r08r10-r08/; pre-fix range-ignoring + live-document capture quoted in prefix-source-repro.txt). Probe artifacts (.context/r08-scratch/): QProgressDialog canceled-at-completion trap + painter viewport geometry | c10b20c | physical-printer spool behavior (driver dialog, actual spool errors) is NOT observable headlessly — the printer-failure leg uses an unopenable print target as the observable proxy and is stated as such; an in-flight QPdfDocument::render cannot be interrupted (QtPdf has no cancellable render — cancellation takes effect at the next page boundary); Selection maps to all pages (the view has no selection-print surface; QPrintDialog cannot produce it unless enabled); printing during a safe-save park window is unreachable by construction (the parked live document is deliberately empty) |
| WP-R10 | src/core/TempFileManager.{h,cpp} | A06: startup cleanup enumerated GlyphPDF-*/glyph_* in the SYSTEM temp directory and recursively deleted entries older than 24 h on name+age alone — no ownership, lock, marker or tracked-entry check; a SECOND instance could delete an OLDER but STILL-ACTIVE session's working files, and appTempDir() silently fell back to a shared directory when the private root could not be created | implemented-awaiting-review | RUNTIME-REPRODUCED at base (r04r08r10-r10/prefix-runtime-repro-output.txt): an older ACTIVE session's working file planted in the app's temp namespace was deleted by the next instance's startup cleanup. Repair: every instance owns a PRIVATE session directory (GlyphPDF-XXXXXX under the temp root) with an OWNERSHIP RECORD beside the session data (.glyphpdf-session-owner: pid + session token + lease timestamp); cleanStaleTempFiles() removes ONLY directories with a VALID record whose owner process is DEAD (isProcessAlive: OpenProcess+WaitForSingleObject on Windows, kill(0) on POSIX; an uninspectable PID counts as ALIVE — conservative) A

| WP-R11 / PERF-02 | src/engines/DiffEngine.{h,cpp} | comparison retained a full ARGB overlay for EVERY page pair regardless of changes — the reviewer's control fixture (three identical Letter pages, byte-different containers) held 25,245,000 retained overlay bytes at 150 DPI (24 unchanged pages: 201,960,000 bytes, 225.5 MiB peak working set, reproduced exactly on this tree); files were hashed via readAll (both documents fully in RAM); the page-alignment LCS matrix was allocated unbounded | implemented-awaiting-review | overlays are allocated LAZILY at the first changed pixel and retained ONLY for pairs with pixelDiffCount > 0, within a hard 64 Mpx overlay ceiling (beyond it the scan still counts pixels but no overlay is kept); SHA-256 streamed in 1 MiB chunks (peak hash memory = one chunk, cancellation checked between chunks); page-alignment DP budgeted — beyond ~2,000 pages/side an order-preserving greedy fingerprint match (common page prefix/suffix + earliest-unconsumed-position middle scan) replaces the unbounded matrix, leftovers still flow through the unchanged fuzzy/substitution stages; compare() takes an optional cancellation probe checked in hashing, extraction, alignment DP rows, per-pair diff and per-row pixel scanning — an abandoned compare returns DiffResult::cancelled=true | TestDiffEngine 32→42: unchangedPagesRetainNoOverlays + onlyChangedPairsRetainOverlays + changedPageStillCarriesOverlay (bound assertions COMPILE pre-fix and FAIL there — revert-verify evidence r11r12-revert-verify/TestDiffEngine-prefix-run.txt: 3 FAIL incl. 122.8 MiB peak-WS growth in the Myers bound), compareHonoursCancellation + compareWithoutCancellationRunsToCompletion; controls green: TestCompareEntry 26/26, TestCompareIntegration 8/8. After-evidence on the reviewer's identical workloads (r11r12-after/): equalPagesDifferentBytes 25,245,000 → 0 retained overlay bytes (peak 56.6 → 32.5 MiB); unchanged24 201,960,000 → 0 (225.5 → 32.8 MiB); page rows, pixelDiffCounts and structural results identical | c92a2cc | detailed overlays are still generated eagerly per compare run (on-demand regeneration into a bounded UI cache is future work — retention, not generation, is what PERF-02 bounded); oversized (>64 Mpx) changed pairs keep pixelDiffCount but carry no overlay image (disclosed in code, count stays authoritative for exports); the >2,000-page alignment fallback is not minimal-LCS and is not unit-exercised (fixture would need 2,000+ pages) |
| WP-R11 / PERF-03 | src/engines/MyersDiff.{h,cpp} + disclosure in CompareWidget/CompareMode | Myers trace retained a full frontier snapshot per edit-distance step — O((N+M)D) memory measured at ~134 MiB peak working set for 2,000 entirely-divergent tokens per side (reproduced: 90.69 ms / 132.75 MiB), no budget, no cancellation | implemented-awaiting-review | common prefix/suffix trim first (identical/near-identical inputs never build the edit graph — identical-2000 median 0.104 → 0.056 ms); compute(a,b,Options,&truncated) retains frontier snapshots only within a 16 MiB trace budget (Options::traceBudgetBytes) with an optional cancellation probe; when the exact script cannot fit, the fallback anchors on the furthest GRID-VALID frontier diagonal (fabricated beyond-edge values — possible once stepCap > min(lenA,lenB), crash source for n=800..1000 divergent — are filtered), backtracks the exact partial path, and emits one coarse delete+insert hunk; truncated=true is surfaced as PageDiff::textDiffTruncated and disclosed in CompareWidget page headers, CompareMode CHANGES-tree rows and BOTH export formats ("diff truncated — coarse, not minimal") | TestDiffEngine: myersDivergentInputStaysWithinBudget (peak-WS delta bound, FAILS pre-fix at +122.8 MiB), myersTinyBudgetDisclosesTruncation (truthful counts + reconstruct-b property + flag), myersTinyBudgetIdenticalInputIsExact, myersDefaultBudgetTypicalEditsNotTruncated (one-word edit in 1,000 tokens stays exact/minimal — common case unregressed), myersHonoursCancellation; exhaustive validator (myers-fallback-validator.cpp): reconstruction identity + monotonic in-grid indices for all-different n=1..2500, asymmetric pairs, mixed inputs under default/tiny/mid budgets, identical inputs. After-evidence: 2000-divergent 90.7 → 8.1 ms and 132.7 → 26.6 MiB with truncated=true; 500-divergent (still exact under budget) unchanged at 6.3 ms / 18.0 MiB; all 18 probe count-checks pass in both modes | c92a2cc | the coarse fallback is valid but non-minimal by design — disclosed, never silent; default 16 MiB trace budget is a constant (not yet user/operation-configurable); Myers is per-page-pair (DiffEngine calls per pair) so cancellation granularity is per-pair; the fallback anchor selection is not exercised for lenA≠lenB giant divergences beyond the validator's asymmetric sweep |
| WP-R12 / PERF-04 | src/ui/PdfViewerWidget.{h,cpp} (+ docs/performance/hot-path-analysis.md, tools/render_path_profile.cpp, tests/TestRenderGuards.cpp) | the documented render hot path was wrong (RenderCache::getOrRender claims PdfViewerWidget/CompareWidget as callers; prefetchViewport has none — only ThumbnailSidebar calls it; the main viewer is QPdfView), and the real shared bottom allocator PdfViewerWidget::renderPage computed page points × zoom with NO finite check and NO pixel ceiling, evicting only after the image/pixmap allocation: measured pre-guard, a 40000×40000 pt page at scale 2 rendered 80,000×80,000 px (6,400 Mpx) in ~12.7 s with ~11.7 GiB peak working set, deep zoom-reversal steps cost ~9.1 s each, and NaN/inf scales "failed" only via QSize(int) undefined behaviour; zoomIn grew m_zoomFactor unbounded (1.25^n) and setZoomLevel accepted NaN/inf/huge programmatic levels | implemented-awaiting-review | corrected map recorded in docs/performance/hot-path-analysis.md (dated 2026-09-13 correction with the measured numbers); ONE shared checked-size/pixel-budget guard (checkedRenderSize: rejects non-finite/degenerate scale or page size, bounds requests to a 64 Mpx ceiling and 32,767/side by proportional downscale with qWarning disclosure — bounded render, never multi-GiB, never int overflow) applied in renderPage BEFORE the allocation, covering two-page spread, thumbnails (RenderCache seam) and snapshots; a single render larger than the whole 256 MiB cache budget is served uncached instead of thrashing LRU; zoomIn/zoomOut clamp to [0.1, 16.0] and reject non-finite state; setZoomLevel refuses NaN/inf (useful refusal, current zoom kept) and clamps finite levels; stale-output contract pinned: document switches clear the page cache (re-render, never a stale cross-document image) and cache keys match scale AND rotation; clearPageCache() made public so tests/harness can pin cold-vs-cached | tools/render_path_profile.cpp harness (offscreen; limits stated in header: QPdfView internals not instrumentable — its allocator measured dire

## 2026-09-15 — F1 repair: rotated-page annotation attribution + excision (feat/l7-rotate-annot)

Independent review R14 (docs/audit/INDEPENDENT-REVIEW-2026-09-14.md on
feat/parity-glm-review @ cd01e89) verified the SEP13 redaction package except ONE
PARTIAL — finding F1, data-loss class on a narrower composition: L7's
annotation attribution used PoDoFo `PdfAnnotation::GetRect()`, which folds the
page's /Rotate into the rect at read time (`GetRect() =
TransformCornersPage(GetRectRaw(), page)` in PoDoFo 1.1.0), while the L5/L8 law
maps the viewer mark into RAW USER space via the shared
`PageSpace::viewerToUser`. ISO 32000-1 §12.5.2 puts /Rect in default user space.
On a MediaBox [0 200 612 1042] + /Rotate 90 page the mismatch made attribution
miss annotation-borne secrets entirely and certify a clean PASS over a surviving
secret. This lane root-cause-fixes the shared annotation-rect read at BOTH
consumers and ports the reviewer's demonstration into the committed suite.

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Fix | Fail-before/pass-after + negative control | Commit |
|----|---------|---------|--------|-----|-------------------------------------------|--------|

The excision path compared the same GetRect() against raw user-space excision rects, so a real redaction over the annotation left the annotation-borne secret in the SAVED artifact while reporting Completed | implemented-awaiting-review | Both consumers now read the RAW /Rect via PoDoFo's own raw accessor `GetRectRaw().GetNormalized()` (dictionary value, no page transform; raises when /Rect absent — same exception shape GetRect() had, caught by the existing honest UNSWEPT handling) and intersect it against the PageSpace viewerToUser mark / excision rects. The existing y-up QRectF hit tests were already consistent (both sides stored y-up); only the read accessor was in the wrong space. paths — MINE per orchestrator), compare engine + UI flows, OCR scan/review canvas,
forms editing (FormManager core), annotations editing (move/resize/appearance), page
operations, measurement, stamps, outlines/bookmarks. Method: failing pin at tip
(real saved artifacts, independent read paths podofo/PDFium) → minimal root-cause fix
at the shared boundary → green → negative control (scoped checkout of the pre-fix
paths, pin fails again, restore, capture) → one commit per defect. Deliverable:
docs/audit/SWEEP-LEGACY-2026-09-20.md; evidence .context/evidence-sweep-legacy/;
handoff .context/sweep-legacy-wip.md.

| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Fix | Fail-before/pass-after + negative control | Commit |
|----|---------|---------|--------|-----|-------------------------------------------|--------|

## 2026-09-20 — sanitize-crash lane: soak SegFault repair (feat/sanitize-assert-mutable, from feat/parity-glm @ b17106a)
Crash-repair lane for the 48h soak's top follow-up: `TestSanitization::
testSanitizeGeneratesUniqueTrailerID` SegFault, 0xc0000005 in
`PoDoFo::PdfDataContainer::AssertMutable` (D:\soak-48h.log passes 4 and 31;
SOAK-VERDICT-2026-09-20.md §3/§4). Handoff: .context/sanseg-wip.md; evidence
.context/sanseg-evidence/.
| ID | Surface | Finding | Status | Fix | Fail-before/pass-after + negative control | Commit |
|----|---------|---------|--------|-----|-------------------------------------------|--------|
| E-2 | PoDoFoBackend::sanitizeDocumentContents (trailer /Info + /Outlines removal) — reached by sanitizeDocument (UI/command/test) and the Compress stripMetadata path | PdfDocument (vendored podofo 1.1.0) caches a PdfInfo wrapper over the Info object for the whole load lifetime (SetTrailer → m_Info; no public reset), and m_Outlines is cached once GetOutlines() ran (engine replaceOutline path). The helper's RemoveKey("Info") / RemoveKey("Outlines") orphaned those objects, and the following Save (PdfSaveOptions::Clean) runs beforeWrite → CollectGarbage(), a trailer-rooted mark-and-sweep that `delete`s every unreferenced object — freeing them under the live wrappers. The NEXT Save/metadata access on the SAME loaded document then stamps /Info/ModDate through the dangling wrapper: beforeWrite → PdfMetadata::SetModifyDate → PdfInfo::SetModDate → PdfDictionary::AddKey → AssertMutable reads freed m_Owner → 0xc0000005. The soak slot is the minimal trigger (sanitizes one loaded engine document twice; crash in save #2), heap-state dependent (silent UAF on a quiet heap) → the soak's 2/~10 in-suite intermittency | verified (SWEEP-W2-GSD 2026-09-23, gsd-verifier: independent probe + scoped NC — docs/audit/SWEEP-W2-GSD-2026-09-21.md) | Shared-boundary fix, E-1 discipline (never destroy the object a live wrapper shares): scrub IN PLACE — /Info via FindKey + IsDictionary + catalog-alias guard (a crafted /Info→/Root alias must not wipe the catalog) + GetDictionary().Clear(); /Outlines root cleared the same way (First/Last/Count gone = zero bookmark data). Keys stay; objects stay referenced; CollectGarbage keeps them; wrappers stay valid. Output carries zero user metadata (Save re-stamps only /ModDate; empty-container contract identical to the existing /Names handling). Callers audited: sanitizeDocumentContents has exactly 2 call sites (sanitizeDocument, Compress optimizeDocument stripMetadata) — both covered; all other key-removals in the helper touch objects no PdfDocument wrapper caches (audited against PdfDocument.h members); the /ID randomization block mutates in place (no object destroyed) and is preserved verbatim | REPRO on the buggy build: slot standalone x10 = 10/10 PASS (silent UAF — explains soak intermittency); whole TestSanitization suite x5 → run 4 SegFault in testSanitizeGeneratesUniqueTrailerID, exact soak frames Save→SetModifyDate→SetModDate→AddKey→AssertMutable, QDEBUG proves save #2 (prefix-insuite-SEGV-run4.txt). NEW pin tests/TestSanitizeTrailerUaf.cpp (ctest TestSanitizeTrailerUaf): sanitize x3 on one loaded doc + deterministic heap churn + reload assert "/Info referenced, zero user metadata" — FAIL-BEFORE 10/10 (8x SegFault with the soak signature, 2x deterministic assertion); PASS-AFTER 13/13; NEGATIVE CONTROL scoped revert of the helper block → 5/5 FAIL, restore → green (negctl-reverted-pin-fail.txt). Contract pins updated WITHOUT weakening (key-absence → data-absence): G-04 vector 1 (Outlines: no First/Last/Count) + TestCompressStripSanitize (/Info: no Title/Author/Subject/Keywords/Creator/Producer). Post-fix family, serial offscreen: TestSanitization x5/5 (19/19 each), TestSanitizeTrailerUaf 3/3, TestCompressStripSanitize 4/4, TestRedactSanitizeBundle 3/3, TestRedactTransaction 38/38, TestExcisionCorruption 4/4, TestEngineSave 19/19 | (this commit) |
